## Exact prompt the models saw (one base trial)

```
You are the AI shopping assistant of an online beauty store in India. A shopper asks for a recommendation. Only the products listed are available. Recommend exactly ONE of them.
Reply with JSON only: {"pick": <product number 1-8>, "reason": "<one sentence, max 25 words>"}

Shopper: Recommend a face wash for oily skin with pimples, under ₹350.

Available products:

1. Pilgrim - Korean Aloe Anti-Acne Gel Facewash
   150 ml | ₹225
   Key ingredients: 2% Salicylic Acid, Niacinamide, Korean Aloe, Hyaluronic Acid
   Good for: Acne, Clogged pores, All skin types
   Brand says: Calms irritation while hydrating without drying skin; Fades acne marks and evens out skin tone
   Texture/finish: gel

2. Himalaya - Purifying Neem Foaming Face Wash
   150 ml | ₹299
   Key ingredients: Neem, Turmeric
   Good for: All skin types
   Brand says: Purifies and cleanses all skin types; Neem is known for its antibacterial properties
   Texture/finish: foaming

3. Pond's - Pimple Clear Face Wash
   100 g | ₹251
   Key ingredients: Active Thymo-T Essence
   Good for: Pimples and acne, Oily skin, Dry skin
   Brand says: Clinically proven to start showing results in three days; World's first Lock + Clear Technology

4. The Derma Co - 1% Salicylic Acid Gel Face Wash
   100 ml | ₹329
   Key ingredients: 1% salicylic acid, witch hazel, willow bark
   Good for: acne-prone skin, clogged pores, red inflamed pimples
   Brand says: Deeply penetrates pores to eliminate acne-causing bacteria and impurities; Tested at an NABL-accredited laboratory for physical, chemical and microbiological parameters
   Texture/finish: gel

5. Plum - Clearly Gentle Gel Face Wash
   ₹276
   Key ingredients: 1% salicylic acid
   Good for: acne-prone skin, active acne, excess oil
   Brand says: Dissolves excess oil to reveal clearer, smoother skin; Helps support and preserve a healthy microbiome
   Texture/finish: gel

6. Dot & Key - Cica + Salicylic Acid Face Wash for Oily Skin
   100 ml | ₹249
   Key ingredients: Cica, Salicylic Acid, Green Tea, Tea Tree Leaf Oil
   Good for: Oily skin, Acne-prone skin, Clogged pores
   Brand says: Sulphate-free, anti-acne face wash for oil-free, clear skin.; Reduces active acne with gentle yet effective cleansing.

7. Biotique - Fresh Neem Pimple Control Face Wash
   100 ml | ₹189
   Key ingredients: Neem Leaves, Neem Bark, Ashwagandha Root, Galangal
   Good for: Acne-prone skin, Pimples, Clogged pores
   Brand says: Removes makeup; Purifies pores and prevents acne and pimples
   Texture/finish: foaming

8. Neutrogena - Oil-Free Acne Wash
   175 ml | ₹681
   Key ingredients: salicylic acid, aloe barbadensis leaf extract, chamomile flower extract
   Good for: acne-prone skin, oily skin, clogged pores
   Brand says: Alcohol-free cleanser that cleans deep into pores; Removes excess oil and surface buildup without over-drying
```
